    Management Discussion and Analysis

    INDUSTRY REVIEW                                                      行業回顧

    New waves of the COVID-19 pandemic continued to emerge               新一波新冠肺炎疫情於二零二一年繼續在
    across the globe in 2021, casting continued uncertainty              全球肆虐，為全球經濟環境帶來不確定
    over the pace of global economic recovery. However, with             性。儘管如此，在高疫苗接種率及嚴格的
    a high vaccination rate and the implementation of stringent          防疫措施推動下，中國的經濟於年內持續
    anti-pandemic measures, the “PRC” sustained continuous and           穩定復甦，國內生產總值按年增長8.1%。
    steady economic recovery during the year, recording an 8.1%
    year-on-year increase in GDP.

    In order to address the impacts of global climate change, the        為應對全球氣候變化的影響，中國政府已
    PRC government has set the “dual-carbon” goals – achieving           設定「雙碳」目標，到二零三零年實現二
    peak carbon dioxide emissions by 2030 and carbon neutrality          氧化碳排放達峰，到二零六年實現碳中
    by 2060. As highlighted in the 2021 Government Work                  和。正如《2021 年政府工作報告》和「十
    Report and the 14th Five-Year Plan, taking solid steps toward        四五」規劃所強調，實現「雙碳」目標已被
    the“dual-carbon” goals has been listed as one of the key             列為主要優先事項之一。二零二一年下半
    priorities. In the second half of 2021, the Central Committee        年，中共中央及國務院發布了《關於完整
    of the Chinese Communist Party and the State Council                 準確全面貫徹新發展理念做好碳達峰碳中
    published the Working Guidance for Carbon Dioxide Peaking            和工作的意見》 和《2023年前碳達峰行動
    and Carbon Neutrality in Full and Faithful Implementation of the     方案》，形成了基本的政策框架，並為實
    New Development Philosophy and the Action Plan for Carbon            現兩個關鍵的減碳目標制定了重要具體目
    Dioxide Peaking Before 2030, which formed the basic policy           標和措施。特別是，政府制定目標將單位
    framework and laid out key specific targets and measures for         國內生產總值耗能和二氧化碳排放較二零
    reaching the two key carbon reduction targets. In particular, the    二零年分別減少13.5%和18%，這將為環
    government set reduction targets of 13.5% and 18% for energy         保行業帶來巨大的潛在商機。
    consumption and carbon dioxide emissions, respectively, per
    unit of GDP from the 2020 level. This, in turn, will present great
    potential business opportunities to the EP industry.

CORPORATE GOVERNANCE PRACTICES                                    企業管治常規

The Board of Directors of the Company (the “Board”) is pleased    本公司董事會（「董事會」）欣然提呈本集
to present the Corporate Governance Report in the Group’s         團截至二零二一年十二月三十一日止年度

The Board was committed to achieving high corporate               董事會致力於達致高水平的企業管治。
governance standards.

The Board believes that high corporate governance standards       董事會認為，高水平的企業管治對於為本
are essential in providing a framework for the Group to           集團提供保障股東（「股東」）權益、提升
safeguard the interests of shareholders (the “Shareholders”),     其企業價值、制定其業務策略和政策以及
enhance corporate value, formulate its business strategies and    提高其透明度及問責性的框架而言至關重
policies, and enhance its transparency and accountability.        要。

Exchange of Hong Kong Limited (the “Stock Exchange”) (the          則條文編製。

Group. The Board considers that the balance of power and            會削弱權力制衡，而此架構將有助本公司

this structure will enable the Company to make and implement

RISK MANAGEMENT AND INTERNAL                                          風險管理及內部監控
CONTROLS

The Board acknowledges its responsibility for the risk                董事會確認知悉其負責風險管理及內部監
management and internal control systems and reviewing their           控系統並檢討其成效之責任。該等系統旨
effectiveness. Such systems are designed to manage rather             在管理而非消除未能達成業務目標的風
than eliminate the risk of failure to achieve business objectives,    險，而且只能就不會有重大的失實陳述或
and can only provide reasonable and not absolute assurance            損失作出合理而非絕對的保證。
against material misstatement or loss.

The Board has the overall responsibility for evaluating and           董事會全面負責評估及釐定本公司達成策
determining the nature and extent of the risks it is willing to       略目標時所願意接納的風險性質及程度，
take in achieving the Company’s strategic objectives, and             並設立和維持合適而有效的風險管理及內
establishing and maintaining appropriate and effective risk           部監控系統。
management and internal control systems.

The Audit Committee assists the Board in leading the                  審核委員會協助董事會開展管理工作並監
management and overseeing their design, implementation and            督董事會設計、實施及監管風險管理及內
monitoring of the risk management and internal control systems.       部監控系統。

management procedures and guidelines with defined                     指引，並通過明晰關鍵業務流程及部門職
authority for implementation by key business processes and            能（包括項目管理、銷售及租賃、財務匯
office functions, including project management, sales and             報、人力資源及信息技術）訂明操作權責。
leasing, financial reporting, human resources and information
technology.

     Pan Asia Environmental Protection Group Limited (the                  泛亞環保集團有限公司（「本公司」）
     “Company”) was incorporated as an exempted company                    於開曼群島註冊成立為一間獲豁免
     and registered in the Cayman Islands with limited liability           有限公司，其股份於香港聯合交易
     and its shares are listed on The Stock Exchange of Hong               所有限公司（「聯交所」）上市。本公
     Kong Limited (the “Stock Exchange”). The addresses of                 司之註冊辦事處及主要營業地點地
     the registered office and principal place of business of              址披露於本年報「公司資料」一節。
     the Company are disclosed in the “Corporate Information”
     section to this annual report.

     The Company and its subsidiaries (collectively referred               本公司及其附屬公司（以下統稱「本
     to as the “Group”) are principally engaged in the sales of            集團」）主要在中華人民共和國（「中
     environmental protection (“EP”) products and equipment,               國」）從事環境保護（「環保」）產品及
     and undertaking of EP construction engineering services               設備之銷售，並承接環保建設工程
     in the People’s Republic of China (the “PRC”), and                    服務及投資控股。
     investment holding.

           b)    Basis of preparation of the consolidated financial              b)   綜合財務報表之編製基準
                 statements

                 The consolidated financial statements for the year                   截至二零二一年十二月三十一
                 ended 31 December 2021 comprise the Company                          日止年度綜合財務報表包括本
                 and its subsidiaries.                                                公司及其附屬公司之財務報
                                                                                      表。

                 The measurement basis used in the preparation of                     綜合財務報表之編製乃以歷史
                 the consolidated financial statements is the historical              成本作計量基準，惟若干金融
                 cost basis except for certain financial instruments,                 工具以公平值計量除外（見下
                 which are stated at their fair value as explained in the             文會計政策所闡釋）。
                 accounting policies set out below.

                 The preparation of consolidated financial statements                 編製符合香港財務報告準則之
                 in conformity with HKFRSs requires management                        綜合財務報表時，管理層需作
                 to make judgements, estimates and assumptions                        出判斷、估計和假設，該等判
                 that affect the application of policies and reported                 斷、估計及假設對會計政策之
                 amounts of assets, liabilities, income and expenses.                 應用以及所呈報資產、負債、
                 The estimates and associated assumptions are                         收入和支出之金額構成影響。
                 based on historical experience and various other                     該等估計及有關假設乃根據過
                 factors believed to be reasonable under the                          往經驗及被認為在該情況下屬
                 circumstances, the results of which form the basis of                合理之多項其他因素而作出，
                 making the judgements about the carrying amounts                     其結果便成為判斷未能透過其
                 of assets and liabilities that are not readily apparent              他來源確定之資產及負債之賬
                 from other sources. Actual results may differ from                   面值之基礎。實際結果可能有
                 these estimates.                                                     別於該等估計。

                 The estimates and underlying assumptions are                         該等估計及相關假設會按持續
                 reviewed on an ongoing basis. Revisions to                           基準進行檢討。對會計估計進
                 accounting estimates are recognised in the period in                 行修訂時，倘修訂僅影響該期
                 which the estimate is revised if the revision affects                間，則修訂會於修訂估計之期
                 only that period, or in the period of the revision and               間確認；或倘有關修訂同時影
                 future periods if the revision affects both current and              響當前及未來期間，則會於修
                 future periods.                                                      訂期間及未來期間確認。

                 Judgements made by management in the application                     管理層在應用對本綜合財務報

2.   SIGNIFICANT ACCOUNTING POLICIES                                  2.   主要會計政策（續）
     (Continued)

     c)   Subsidiaries                                                     c)   附屬公司

          Subsidiaries are entities controlled by the Group. The                附屬公司指受本集團控制之實
          Group controls an entity when it is exposed, or has                   體。當本集團承受或享有由參
          rights, to variable returns from its involvement with                 與實體所得不定回報時，且有
          the entity and has the ability to affect those returns                能力透過其對實體之權力影響
          through its power over the entity. When assessing                     該等回報時，則本集團控制該
          whether the Group has power, only substantive                         實體。於評估控制權時，惟本
          rights (held by the Group and other parties) are                      集團及其他人士持有之實質性
          considered.                                                           權利計算在內。

          An investment in a subsidiary is consolidated into                    於附屬公司之投資將由控制權
          the consolidated financial statements from the date                   開始至控制權終止之期間計入
          that control commences until the date that control                    綜合財務報表。集團內部往來
          ceases. Intra-group balances, transactions and cash                   之餘額、交易及現金流量以及
          flows and any unrealised profits arising from intra-                  由集團內部交易產生之任何未
          group transactions are eliminated in full in preparing                變現溢利，均在編製綜合財務
          the consolidated financial statements. Unrealised                     報表時全數抵銷。集團內部交
          losses resulting from intra-group transactions are                    易所產生之未變現虧損，其抵
          eliminated in the same way as unrealised gains                        銷方法與抵銷未變現收益之方
          but only to the extent that there is no evidence of                   法相同，但抵銷額只限於沒有
          impairment.                                                           證據顯示已出現減值之部份。

          Changes in the Group’s interests in a subsidiary that                 本集團於一間附屬公司之權益
          do not result in a loss of control are accounted for as               變 動（ 並 無 導 致 失 去 控 制 權 ）
          equity transactions, whereby adjustments are made                     列作權益交易，並對合併權益
          to the amounts of controlling and non-controlling                     中的控股及非控股權益金額作
          interests within consolidated equity to reflect the                   出調整，以反映有關權益的變
          change in relative interests, but no adjustments are                  動，惟概無對商譽作出任何調
          made to goodwill and no gain or loss is recognised.                   整及並無確認任何損益。

          When the Group loses control of a subsidiary, it is                   倘本集團失去一間附屬公司的
          accounted for as a disposal of the entire interest in                 控制權，將列作出售其於該附
          that subsidiary, with a resulting gain or loss being                  屬公司的全部權益，因此產生
          recognised in profit or loss. Any interest retained in                的收益或虧損於損益中確認。
          that former subsidiary at the date when control is                    於失去控制權當日仍保留於前
          lost is recognised at fair value and this amount is                   附屬公司的任何權益乃按公平
          regarded as the fair value on initial recognition of a                值確認，而該金額將視為於初
          financial asset, or, when appropriate, the cost on                    步確認金融資產時或（如適用）
          initial recognition of an investment in an associate or               就於聯營公司或合營企業之投
          a joint venture.                                                      資進行初步確認時之成本之公
                                                                                平值。

          In the Company’s statement of financial position,                     在本公司財務狀況表中，於附
          an investment in a subsidiary is stated at cost less                  屬公司之投資以成本減去減值
          impairment losses (see note 2(f)(ii)), unless the                     虧損列賬（見附註 2(f)(ii)），除
          investment is classified as held for sale (or included                非該投資被列為持作出售（或
          in a disposal group that is classified as held for sale).             計入分類為持作出售之出售組
                                                                                別）。

                 Depreciation is calculated to write off the cost of                 物業、廠房及設備項目乃使用
                 items of property, plant and equipment, less their                  直線法按其估計可使用年期減
                 estimated residual value, if any, using the straight-               彼等之估計剩餘價值（如有）以
                 line method over their estimated useful lives.                      撇銷其成本值計算折舊。

                 Furniture, fixtures and equipment               5 years             傢俬、裝置及設備          五年
                 Leasehold improvements                1 year to 5 years             租賃物業裝修          一至五年
                 Motor vehicles                                  5 years             汽車                五年
                 Machineries                                    10 years             機器                十年

                 Where parts of an item of property, plant and                       當一項物業、廠房及設備之各
                 equipment have different useful lives, the cost of the              部份有不同之可使用年期，此
                 item is allocated on a reasonable basis between the                 項目各部份之成本將按合理基
                 parts and each part is depreciated separately. Both                 礎分配，而每部份將作個別折
                 the useful life of an asset and its residual value, if              舊。資產之可使用年期及其剩
                 any, are reviewed annually.                                         餘 價 值（ 如 有 ）會 每 年 進 行 檢
                                                                                     討。

                 Gains or losses arising from the retirement or                      因報廢或出售物業、廠房及設

                 item and are recognised in profit or loss on the date               於報廢或出售當日於損益內確
                 of retirement or disposal.                                          認。

           e)    Leased assets                                                  e)   租賃資產

                 At inception of a contract, the Group assesses                      訂立合同時，本集團評估該合
                 whether the contract is, or contains, a lease. A                    同是否屬於租賃或包含租賃。
                 contract is, or contains, a lease if the contract                   如果合同授予權利以代價為交
                 conveys the right to control the use of an                          換在某一時期內控制已識別
                 identified asset for a period of time in exchange                   資產的使用的權利，則該合同
                 for consideration. Control is conveyed where the                    屬於租賃或包含租賃。當客戶
                 customer has both the right to direct the use of the                有權主導已識別資產的使用及
                 identified asset and to obtain substantially all of the             有權從使用中獲取幾乎所有的
                 economic benefits from that use.                                    經濟利益時，則表示擁有控制
                                                                                     權。

          capitalise the lease on a lease-by-lease basis. The                   賃基準將租賃資本化。與該等

          a systematic basis over the lease term.                               開支。

          using the interest rate implicit in the lease or, if that             利率貼現或，倘該利率難以
          rate cannot be readily determined, using a relevant                   釐定，則採用相關增量借款利
          incremental borrowing rate. After initial recognition,                率計算。於初步確認後，租賃
          the lease liability is measured at amortised cost and                 負債按攤銷成本計量且利息開

          charged to profit or loss in the accounting period in                 會計期間計入損益。
          which they are incurred.

               including historical experience and forward-                                團會考慮合理可靠的定

                     will not be reduced below its individual fair                        位（ 或 一 組 現 金 產

           g)    Inventories                                                   g)   存貨

                 Inventories are carried at the lower of cost and                   存貨按成本及可變現淨值兩者
                 net realisable value. Cost is calculated using the                 之較低者列賬。成本以加權平
                 weighted average cost formula and comprises all                    均成本方程式計算，包括使存

                 bringing the inventories to their present location and             之購買成本及其他成本。
                 condition.

                 Net realisable value is the estimated selling price in             可變現淨值乃按日常業務過程
                 the ordinary course of business less the estimated                 中之估計售價減完成之估計成
                 costs of completion and the estimated costs                        本及進行銷售所需之估計成本
                 necessary to make the sale.                                        計算。

                 When inventories are sold, the carrying amount of                  所出售存貨的賬面值在相關收
                 those inventories is recognised as an expense in the               入獲確認的期間內確認為支
                 period in which the related revenue is recognised.                 出。存貨撇減至可變現淨值的
                 The amount of any write-down of inventories to net                 金額及存貨的所有虧損，均在

                 down or loss occurs. The amount of any reversal                    之金額在出現撥回期間內確認

                 as an expense in the period in which the reversal

           o)    Income tax                                                      o)   所得稅

                 Income tax for the year comprises current tax and                    本年度所得稅包括即期稅項及
                 movements in deferred tax assets and liabilities.                    遞延稅項資產及負債之變動。
                 Current tax and movements in deferred tax assets                     即期稅項及遞延稅項資產及負
                 and liabilities are recognised in profit or loss except              債之變動均在損益內確認，惟
                 to the extent that they relate to items recognised                   彼等有關於其他全面收益或直
                 in other comprehensive income or directly in                         接於權益確認之項目除外，於
                 equity, in which case the relevant amounts of tax                    此情況下，有關稅款分別於其
                 are recognised in other comprehensive income or                      他全面收益確認或直接於權益
                 directly in equity, respectively.                                    確認。

                 Current tax is the expected tax payable on the                       即期稅項乃按年內應課稅收入
                 taxable income for the year, using tax rates enacted                 根據報告期末已執行或實質上
                 or substantively enacted at the end of the reporting                 已執行之稅率計算之預期應付
                 period, and any adjustment to tax payable in respect                 稅項，加上過往年度應付稅項
                 of previous years.                                                   之任何調整。

                 Deferred tax assets and liabilities arise from                       遞延稅項資產及負債分別由可
                 deductible and taxable temporary differences                         抵扣及應課稅暫時差異產生，
                 respectively, being the differences between the                      即資產及負債就財務呈報之賬
                 carrying amounts of assets and liabilities for financial             面值與其課稅基礎值之差異。
                 reporting purposes and their tax bases. Deferred tax                 遞延稅項資產亦產生自未動用
                 assets also arise from unused tax losses and unused                  稅項虧損及未動用稅項抵免。
                 tax credits.

                 Apart from certain limited exceptions, all deferred tax              除若干有限之例外情況外，所
                 liabilities, and all deferred tax assets to the extent               有遞延稅項負債及所有遞延稅
                 that it is probable that future taxable profits will be              項資產（以未來可能有應課稅
                 available against which the asset can be utilised, are               溢利可予抵銷之資產為限）均
                 recognised. Future taxable profits that may support                  予以確認。支持確認由可抵扣
                 the recognition of deferred tax assets arising from                  暫時差異所產生之遞延稅項資
                 deductible temporary differences include those                       產之未來應課稅溢利包括因撥
                 that will arise from the reversal of existing taxable                回現有應課稅暫時差異而產生
                 temporary differences, provided those differences                    之數額；惟此等撥回之差異與
                 relate to the same taxation authority and the same                   同一稅務機關及同一應課稅實
                 taxable entity, and are expected to reverse either                   體有關，並預期在可抵扣暫時
                 in the same period as the expected reversal of the                   差異預計撥回之同一期間或遞
                 deductible temporary differences or in periods into                  延稅項資產所產生可抵扣虧損
                 which a tax loss arising from the deferred tax asset                 可向後期或向前期結轉之期間
                 can be carried back or forward. The same criteria are                內撥回。在判斷現有應課稅暫
                 adopted when determining whether existing taxable                    時差異是否足以支持確認因未
                 temporary differences support the recognition of                     動用稅項虧損及稅項抵免所產
                 deferred tax assets arising from unused tax losses                   生的遞延稅項資產時，亦會採
                 and credits, that is, those differences are taken into               用相同準則，即如該等差額與
                 account if they relate to the same taxation authority                相同稅務機關及相同應課稅實
                 and the same taxable entity, and are expected to                     體有關，並預期在稅項虧損或
                 reverse in a period, or periods, in which the tax loss               稅項抵免可被動用的同一期間
                 or credit can be utilised.                                           內撥回的情況下，始會計入有
                                                                                      關差異。

     s)   Borrowing costs                                                s)   借貸成本

          Borrowing costs that are directly attributable to                   購買、建造或生產就其擬作用
          the acquisition, construction or production of an                   途或銷售而需要相當長時間的
          asset which necessarily takes a substantial period                  資產所直接涉及之借貸成本列
          of time to get ready for its intended use or sale are               作該資產成本之一部分。其他
          capitalised as part of the cost of that asset. Other                借貸成本於產生期間支銷。
          borrowing costs are expensed in the period in which
          they are incurred.

          The capitalisation of borrowing costs as part of                    當發生資產開支、產生借貸成
          the cost of a qualifying asset commences when                       本及正進行就其擬作用途或銷
          expenditure for the asset is being incurred, borrowing              售而須準備資產的活動時，則
          costs are being incurred and activities that are                    借貸成本開始資本化為合資
          necessary to prepare the asset for its intended use                 格資產成本之一部分。倘就其
          or sale are in progress. Capitalisation of borrowing                擬作用途或銷售而須準備合資
          costs is suspended or ceases when substantially                     格資產的大部分活動中斷或完
          all the activities necessary to prepare the qualifying              成，則借貸成本暫停或終止資
          asset for its intended use or sale are interrupted or               本化。
          complete.

     Sales of EP products and equipment                                      銷售環保產品及設備

     the installation and on-site testing. The trading terms with            條款以信貸為主，且客戶通常須支

     is normally required from customers. A certain percentage               付款直至保固期結束。與銷售環保

     of EP products and equipment cannot be purchased                        協定規格之保證。
     separately and they serve as an assurance that the

     specifications.

25. SHARE OPTION SCHEME                                              25. 購股權計劃

   The Company’s share option scheme (the “Share Option                本 公 司 之 購 股 權 計 劃（「 購 股 權 計
   Scheme”) was adopted pursuant to a resolution passed                劃」）乃根據於二零零七年十二月一
   on 1 December 2007. The purpose of the Share Option                 日通過之決議案獲採納。購股權計
   Scheme is to enable the Company to grant options to                 劃旨在促使本公司授予經甄選參與
   selected participants as incentives or rewards for their            者購股權，作為彼等為本集團作出
   contributions to the Group. Under the Share Option                  貢獻之獎勵或獎賞。根據購股權計
   Scheme, the directors of the Company may grant options              劃，本公司董事可向下列合資格參
   to the following eligible participants:                             與者授出購股權：

   i)      any employee (whether full-time or part-time,               i)       本公司、其任何附屬公司或本
           including any executive director but excluding any                   集團持有其股權之任何實體
           non-executive director) of the Company, any of its                  （「投資實體」）之任何僱員（不
           subsidiaries or any entity (“Invested Entity”) in which              論全職或兼職，包括任何執行
           the Group holds an equity interest;                                  董事，但不包括任何非執行董
                                                                                事）；

   ii)     any non-executive director (including independent           ii)     本公司、其任何附屬公司或任
           non-executive director) of the Company, any of its                  何投資實體之任何非執行董事
           subsidiaries or any Invested Entity;                                （包括獨立非執行董事）；

   iii)    any supplier of goods or services to any member of          iii)    向本集團任何成員公司或任何

      26. SEGMENT REPORTING                                                26. 分部報告
            The Group manages its business by divisions and all those        本集團按部門劃分管理其業務，所
            divisions are located in the PRC. In a manner consistent         有該等部門均位於中國。為了與就
            with the way in which information is reported internally         資源分配及表現評估向本集團首席
            to the Group’s CODM for the purposes of resources                營運決策人內部呈報之資料方式一
            allocation and performance assessment, the Group’s               致，香港財務報告準則第8號營運分
            operating and reportable segments under HKFRS 8,                 部 項下本集團的營運及可呈報分部
            Operating segments are organised into two main operating         劃分為兩個主要營運分部，包括 ( i )
            segments including (i) EP products and equipment, and (ii)       環保產品及設備，及 (ii) 環保建設工
            EP construction engineering services. No other operating         程服務。於釐定本集團可呈報分部
            segments identified by the CODM have been aggregated             時，概無將首席營運決策人所識別
            in arriving at the reportable segments of the Group.             之其他營運分部合併。

            a)    Segment revenue, profit or loss, assets and                a)   分部收入、溢利或虧損、資產
                  liabilities                                                     及負債

                  For the purposes of assessing segment performance               就評估分部表現及分部間分配
                  and allocating resources between segments, the                  資源而言，本集團之首席營運
                  Group’s CODM monitors the results, assets and                   決策人按以下基準監察各可呈
                  liabilities attributable to each reportable segment on          報分部之業績、資產及負債：
                  the following basis:

                  Segment assets include all tangible and intangible              分部資產包括所有有形、無形
                  assets, and current assets with the exception of                及流動資產（未分配現金及銀
                  unallocated cash and bank balances, investments                 行結餘、金融資產及其他企業
                  in financial assets and other corporate assets.                 資產之投資除外）。分部負 債
                  Segment liabilities include trade and other payables            包括個別分部應佔的應付貿易
                  attributable to individual segments.                            及其他款項。

                  Revenue and expenses are allocated to the                       收益及支出乃經參考該等分部
                  reportable segments with reference to sales                     產生之銷售額及提供之服務，
                  generated and services rendered by those segments               以及所產生之支出或該等分部
                  and the expenses incurred by those segments                     應佔之資產折舊或攤銷產生之
                  or which otherwise arise from the depreciation                  支出分配予該等可呈報分部。
                  or amortisation of assets attributable to those                 分部間提供之支持不計算在
                  segments. Inter-segment assistance provided by one              內。
                  segment to another is not measured.

                  The measure used for reporting segment profit/                  用 於 呈 報 分 部 溢 利 ╱（ 虧 損 ）
                  (loss) is “adjusted EBITDA” that is, “adjusted                  的方法為「經調整 EBITDA」，
                  earnings before interest, taxes, depreciation and               即「 經 調 整 之 未 計 利 息 、 稅
                  amortisation”, where “interest” is regarded as                  項、折舊及攤銷前的盈利」，
                  including investment income and “depreciation and               其 中「 利 息 」包 括 投 資 收 入 ，
                  amortisation” is regarded as including impairment               而「折舊及攤銷」包括非流動資
                  losses on non-current assets. To arrive at adjusted             產之減值虧損。為達至經調整
                  EBITDA, the Group’s loss are further adjusted                   E B I T D A，本集團之虧損乃對
                  for items not specifically attributed to individual             並未指定屬於個別分部之項目
                  segments, such as directors’ emoluments, auditor’s              作出進一步調整，如董事薪酬
                  remuneration and other corporate administration                 及核數師之酬金及其他公司行
                  costs.                                                          政成本。


                  In addition to receiving segment information                    除收到有關經調整 E B I T D A 之
                  concerning adjusted EBITDA, the Group’s CODM                    分部資料外，本集團之首席營
                  is provided with segment information concerning                 運決策人獲提供用於各分部營
                  revenue, depreciation, amortisation and impairment              運有關收入、折舊、攤銷及減
                  losses and additions to non-current segment assets              值虧損以及非流動分部資產添
                  used by the segments in their operations. Inter-                置之分部資料。分部間銷售乃
                  segment sales are priced with reference to prices               經參考就類似訂單向外部人士
                  charged to external parties for similar orders.                 收取之價格而進行定價。

      29. CAPITAL MANAGEMENT                                                29. 資本管理

            The Group’s primary objectives when managing capital               本集團管理資本之首要目標乃保障
            are to safeguard the Group’s ability to continue as                本集團能夠繼續根據持續經營基準
            a going concern, and to generate sufficient profit to              經營，以及產生足夠盈利以維持增
            maintain growth and provide a satisfactory return to its           長，及為股東賺取滿意之回報。
            shareholders.

            The management actively and regularly reviews and                  管理層積極及定期對資本架構進行
            manages its capital structure to maintain a balance                檢討及管理，以在較高股東回報情
            between the higher shareholder returns that might                  況下可能伴隨之較高借貸水平與穩
            be possible with higher levels of borrowings and the               健之資本狀況帶來之好處及保障之
            advantages and security afforded by a sound capital                間取得平衡，並透過對股東派發股
            position, and makes adjustments to the capital structure           息或進行新債務融資就資本架構作
            through the amount of dividend payment to shareholders             出調整。年內，概無就該等目標或
            or raise new debt financing. No changes were made in the           政策作出任何變動。
            objectives or policies during the year.

            The management monitors the Group’s capital structure              管理層按債權比率淨值基準監察本
            on the basis of a net debt-to-equity ratio. For this purpose,      集團之資本結構。為此，本集團界
            the Group defines net debt as total debt (which includes           定債務淨額為債務總額（包括應付貿
            trade and other payables, lease liabilities and corporate          易及其他款項、租賃負債以及公司
            bonds) minus cash and bank balances.                               債券）減現金及銀行結餘。

            There was no net debt for the Group as at 31 December              本集團於二零二一年及二零二零年
            2021 and 2020.                                                     十二月三十一日概無債務淨額。

            Neither the Company nor any of its subsidiaries are                本公司或任何其附屬公司概無受外
            subject to externally imposed capital requirements.                部實施之資本規定限制。

31. FINANCIAL RISK MANAGEMENT AND FAIR                            31. 金融風險管理及金融工具之
    VALUE OF FINANCIAL INSTRUMENTS                                    公平值

   The risks associated with the financial instruments include      與該等金融工具有關之風險包括本
   credit risk, interest rate risk, liquidity risk and currency     集團日常業務過程中產生的信貸風
   risk arises in the normal course of the Group’s business.        險、利率風險、流動資金風險及貨
   The Group’s exposure to these risks and the financial risk       幣風險。本集團所面對之風險及本
   management policies and practices used by the Group to           集團用以管理該等風險的財務風險
   manage these risks are described below.                          管理政策及慣例載列如下。

   a)   Credit risk                                                 a)   信貸風險

        Credit risk refers to the risk that a counterparty will          信貸風險指對手方將違反合同
        default on its contractual obligations resulting in a            責任以致本集團蒙受財務虧
        financial loss to the Group. The Group’s credit risk is          損。本集團之信貸風險主要來
        primarily attributable to trade and other receivables,           自應收貿易及其他款項以及銀
        and bank balances. Management has a credit policy                行結餘。管理層已制定信貸政
        in place and the exposures to these credit risks are             策，並持續監察該等信貸風
        monitored on an ongoing basis.                                   險。

        i)   Trade receivables                                           i)   應收貿易款項

             In respect of trade receivables, individual credit               就應收貿易款項而言，
             evaluations are performed on all customers                       本集團對要求一定金額
             requiring credit over a certain amount. These                    以上信貸之所有客戶個
             evaluations focus on the customer’s past                         別進行信貸評估。該等
             history of making payments when due and                          評估主要根據客戶之歷
             current ability to pay, and take into account                    史如期付款記錄及現時
             information specific to the customer as well                     之償付能力，並考慮客
             as pertaining to the economic environment                        戶特定資料及其所處之
             in which the customer operates. The Group                        經濟環境。本集團一般
             generally allows credit period ranging from 0                    給予貿易客戶 0 至 180 日
             to 180 days to its trade customers. Normally,                    的信貸期。在一般情況
             the Group does not obtain collateral from its                    下，本集團不會自客戶
             customers.                                                       獲取抵押品。

             In respect of trade receivables, the Group’s                     就應收貿易款項而言，
             exposure to credit risk is influenced mainly by                  本集團之信貸風險主要
             the individual characteristics of each customer.                 受每個客戶之個別情況
             Taking into account the creditworthiness of the                  所影響。考慮到本集團
             Group’s customers, the credit risk measures                      客戶之信譽、信貸風險
             and the historical levels of the bad debts,                      措施及過往之壞賬水
             the directors of the Company consider that                       平，本公司董事認為，
             such concentration of credit risk would result                   此種集中信貸風險會對
             in significant credit default exposure to the                    本集團構成重大信貸拖
             Group. At the end of the reporting period, 0%                    欠風險。於報告期末，
             (2020: 49%) and 100% (2020: 49%) of trade                        來自環保產品及設備分
             receivables were due from the Group’s largest                    部之本集團最大客戶及
             customer and the five largest customers,                         五大客戶分別佔應收貿
             respectively, arising from EP products and                       易 款 項 0 %（ 二 零 二 零
             equipment segment.                                               年：49%）及100%（二零

      32. CRITICAL ACCOUNTING JUDGEMENTS                                      32. 關鍵會計判斷及估計不確定
          AND KEY SOURCES OF ESTIMATION                                           性之主要來源
          UNCERTAINTY

            a)    Key sources of estimation uncertainty                         a)   估計不確定性之主要來源

                  In the application of the Group’s accounting policies,             誠如附註 2 所述，在應用本集
                  which are described in note 2, the directors of                    團會計政策時，本公司董事須
                  the Company are required to make judgements,                       對未能透過其他來源確定之資
                  estimates and assumptions about the carrying                       產及負債賬面值作出判斷、估
                  amounts of assets and liabilities that are not readily             計及假設。該等估計及有關假
                  apparent from other sources. The estimates and                     設乃根據過往經驗及其他被視
                  associated assumptions are based on historical                     為相關之因素而作出。實際結
                  experience and other factors that are considered                   果可能有別於該等估計。
                  to be relevant. Actual results may differ from these
                  estimates.

                  The estimates and underlying assumptions are                       該等估計及相關假設會按持續
                  reviewed on an ongoing basis. Revisions to                         基準進行檢討。對會計估計進
                  accounting estimates are recognised in the period in               行修訂時，倘修訂僅影響該期
                  which the estimate is revised if the revision affects              間，則修訂會於修訂估計之期
                  only that period, or in the period of the revision and             間確認；或倘有關修訂同時影
                  future periods if revision affects both current and                響即期及未來期間，則會於修
                  future periods.                                                    訂期間及未來期間確認。

                  i)    Estimation of impairment of trade receivables                i)   應收貿易款項之減值估
                                                                                          計

                         The Group estimates the loss allowances for                      本集團透過評估預期信
                         trade receivables by assessing the ECLs. This                    貸虧損估計應收貿易款
                         requires the use of estimates and judgements.                    項的虧損撥備，需運用
                         ECLs are based on the Group’s historical credit                  估計及判斷。預期信貸
                         loss experience, adjusted for factors that are                   虧損乃根據本集團的
                         specific to the debtors, and an assessment                       過 往 信 貸 虧 損 經 驗（ 就
                         of both the current and forecast general                         債務人特定因素作出調
                         economic conditions at the end of reporting                      整）及於報告期末對當前
                         period. Where the estimation is different from                   及預期整體經濟狀況之
                         the original estimate, such difference will affect               評估而得出。倘估計與
                         the carrying amounts of trade receivables and                    初始估計出現差異，有
                         thus the impairment loss in the period in which                  關差異將影響應收貿易
                         such estimate is changed. The Group keeps                        款項的賬面值，因此，
                         assessing the expected credit loss of trade                      該等估計期間的減值虧
                         receivables during their expected lives. As at 31                損會出現變動。本集團
                         December 2021, the carrying amount of trade                      於應收貿易款項的預期
                         receivables was approximately RMB2,463,000                       存續期內持續對其預期
                         (2020: RMB36,262,000). Details of the                            信貸虧損進行評估。於
                         impairment of trade receivables are disclosed in                 二零二一年十二月三十
                         notes 17 and 31(a).                                              一日，應收貿易款項
                                                                                          的賬面值約為人民幣
                                                                                          2,463,000 元（二零二零
                                                                                          年：人民幣 36,262,000
                                                                                          元）。有關應收貿易款項

                                                                                          17及31(a)。
